# Skills · F · part 29

851 entries, feed-triage to feedback-synthesis. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **feed-triage**: podcastfreaks.com で「サイトの状態」issue が立ったとき、または /errors にエラーや警告が出たときに、原因を調べて直す。フィードが取得できない、音声を持たない、ドメインが第三者に取得された、といった番組の扱いを決める。「フィードのエラー」「番組が取得できない」「site-health の issue」「rss-inactive へ移す」時に使用。 ([noracast/podcastfreaks.com](https://github.com/noracast/podcastfreaks.com/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/feed-triage) · ★48)
- **feed-tuner**: Compares what you bookmark on daily.dev with the tags and sources you follow, then proposes follow, unfollow and block changes and applies only the ones you approve. ([dailydotdev/marketplace](https://github.com/dailydotdev/marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/feed-tuner/skills/feed-tuner) · ★8)
- **feed-tuner**: Keep the user's Upwork job alerts accurate with the flconsole MCP server. Sets up a feed tuner once (target jobs a day, what the user never takes, budget and client limits), then on an hourly schedule checks each feed with Telegram alerts — the jobs against the user's wishes, how many arrive, what the AI filter skipped, the user's 👍/👎 — and makes at most one set of related edits per run. Every change reaches the user in Telegram with an Undo button. Use when the user wants their job alerts tuned or kept accurate automatically, or when a scheduled task says "Run flconsole feed tuner check". ([droopboop/flconsole-upwork-mcp](https://github.com/droopboop/flconsole-upwork-mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/feed-tuner))

- **feedback-synthesis**: Turn raw user feedback into actionable product decisions. Categorization frameworks, signal vs noise filtering, and severity scoring. Use when processing interviews, support tickets, or reviews into product direction. ([Dragoon0x/product-skills](https://github.com/Dragoon0x/product-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/research/feedback-synthesis) · ★4)
- **feedback-synthesis**: Turn raw customer feedback from multiple sources into clear product insights and priorities. Covers feedback collection, pattern recognition, and separating signal from noise. Use when feedback feels overwhelming, contradictory, or when the team can't agree on what users actually want.
